I had filed it incorrectly.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To find something or someone after searching for a long time."],"synonyms":[{"word":"run to earth"},{"word":"run to ground"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You need to wind up the clock every day so that it doesn't run down.","type":"example"},{"text":"If you don't switch off the car lights, you will run the battery down.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To lose power slowly. Used for a machine, battery, or other powered device."],"tags":["intransitive","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Running down the list of suggestions, I can see three we can discard immediately.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To read quickly a list or other short text."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To describe in the form of a rundown, a rough outline or summary."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The board of directors have decided to run down the stocks held in storage prior to offering the company for sale.","type":"example"},{"text":"Before you move, you should run down your food supplies in the fridge, freezer, and pantry to use up as much as possible.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To reduce the size or stock levels of a business, often with a view to closure."],"tags":["UK","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to run down in health","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To decline in quality or condition."]},{"examples":[{"text":"to run down a stag","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To chase till the object pursued is captured or exhausted."],"topics":["hobbies","hunting","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["To run against and sink, as a vessel."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["To crush;
